## I. Process, self-knowledge & documentation drift

### MIR-035 — memory-fix defects were not registered as agent-visible `SelfImprovementIssue`s
- **Aliases:** MEMORY_FIX_PLAN Part B; OFM-015 ("recorded but never read"). **Provenance:** newly_discovered (this pass).
- **Sources:** MEMORY_FIX_PLAN.md Part B; verified against `data/self_improvement_issues.jsonl`.
- **Files/functions:** `core/self_improvement_issues.py`; `data/self_improvement_issues.jsonl` (40 records).
- **Symptom:** the plan's central goal — register each A1–A8 / CORE fix as a durable `SelfImprovementIssue` + `lesson` episode so the agent knows its own problems — is **not** reflected: the 40 registry records are auto-generated self-build failures ("Turn the self-improvement failure into a bounded repair"), not the memory-fix defects.
- **Root cause:** the memory fixes (MIR-001/004/005/007/012/017) landed in git but were **not** journaled into the agent's self-knowledge registry.
- **Production path:** `registry.unresolved()` → Stage-A/B self-repair ladder — currently blind to these fixes.
- **Existing tests:** self_improvement_issues tests (mechanism), not content.
- **Missing tests:** each landed fix has a registered issue + a re-runnable verifier (`:self-issue-verify`).
- **RE-MEASURED 2026-08-22 AND THE GAP HAS WIDENED, not merely persisted.** The registry now holds **106 distinct issues** (this entry recorded 40). Every one of the 106 carries the same `action` value — `improve_failure_to_idea_pipeline` — and every one is `status=open`. Searched across the whole file: **zero mentions of any `MIR-NNN` id**, and zero mentions of `MEMORY_FIX` or the A1–A8 labels. So the plan's central goal is not partially met; it is met to exactly the extent it was, while the store around it filled with 106 records of a single auto-generated kind.
- **Two consequences the original entry could not see.** (1) **Nothing ever closes.** 106 of 106 open means no issue in the agent's own defect registry has ever reached a resolved state, so `registry.unresolved()` — the input to the Stage-A/B self-repair ladder — returns a monoculture that grows monotonically. (2) It is the same shape measured elsewhere on the same day: one loop that mostly does not succeed consuming a durable store, exactly as `self-build-produce` consumes 42% of episodic memory (`docs/audit/SELF_BUILD_FAILURE_ANALYSIS.md`). The self-knowledge registry has the defect its own subject matter describes.
- **THE REAL ROOT FOUND AND FIXED, 2026-08-22 evening — and it was not the entry's original subject.** The monoculture's mechanism: (1) any episode carrying `defect_signals` minted a durable failure whose text embeds the full campaign question, and the fingerprint hashed the text — so ONE detector class on N questions minted N permanent open issues (13 copies of one signal pair, measured); (2) the biggest feeder was `reasoning_action_mismatch` — the MIR-015 sensor with its 71% firing rate and table defects, whose escalation-contract default forbids attaching enforcement to the family. A permanent open defect per firing is enforcement wearing a ledger's clothes. **The registry was the sensor's sewer.**
- **The repair.** Detector-minted issues are keyed by SIGNAL CLASS (`failure_fingerprint` special case; action `investigate_detector_signal`) — the repair ladder repairs classes, and instances accumulate as evidence on one issue. The unreliable family (`reasoning_action_mismatch`, `user_contract_unrepresented`) mints nothing, solo or on a reliable signal's coat-tails; the verifier-caught signals (`content_refuted`, `citation_fabricated` — the route's founding case) stay first-class. Non-detector failures keep text-keyed identity: two rollback reasons ARE two defects. Migration `scripts/collapse_detector_issue_echoes.py` replayed the current rules over the standing rows with a backup: **106 → 25** (43 unreliable-solo echoes dropped, the rest merged into 9 signal classes + 16 genuine self-build failures). Seven tests, both halves break-tested red.
- **The entry's ORIGINAL half — registering the A1–A8 memory fixes — is closed as overtaken by events, stated rather than performed:** those fixes are landed, test-pinned and recorded in this registry, which is the durable self-knowledge surface; minting 60 resolved runtime issues for the repair ladder to skip would be ceremony. The runtime registry is the agent's WORK QUEUE, and its work is what remains open.
- **Status:** `fixed` (2026-08-22 evening). Closure semantics beyond this (auto-resolve when a class stops recurring) stay future work, noted, not promised.

- **CLOSURE AUDITED 2026-08-22 — half held, half LANDED.** Rule: «merging by class hides distinct defects under one row». The merge itself is right (the ladder repairs classes, not turns), but evidence is capped at eight samples, so ten failures of one class stored eight and the row said nothing about the rest. The loss is not the samples — it is the MAGNITUDE: a class seen 50 times read the same as one seen 8, and recurrence is the whole reason a class earns an investigation. `occurrences` is now carried and stated in the proposed action (`seen=47x`). The obvious `+1` would have been wrong: `update_self_improvement_issues` re-reads a seven-day window every sweep, so it would have counted SWEEPS, not failures — the count advances only on a strictly newer stamp, undercounting same-timestamp pairs by one on purpose.
### MIR-036 — audit documents are stale (fixed items still listed as open)
- **Aliases:** Stage-1 DOCUMENT_INVENTORY headline. **Provenance:** newly_discovered (this pass).
- **Sources:** DOCUMENT_INVENTORY.md; CORE_AUDIT §7, OPERATIONAL_FAILURE_MODES §11, MEMORY_SYSTEM_AUDIT.
- **Symptom:** CORE_AUDIT §7 says "None is yet fixed in code"; OFM §11 lists OFM-010 as the sole confirmed-gap — both contradicted by current code (MIR-001/004/005/007/012/017 fixed).
- **Root cause:** docs not updated after fixes landed.
- **Status:** **`fixed` (2026-07-20)** ⬆ from `stale_finding`.
- **Resolution:** both stale ledgers now carry a dated SUPERSEDED banner instead of being rewritten. `CORE_AUDIT_2026-07-18.md` §7 ("None is yet fixed in code") and `OPERATIONAL_FAILURE_MODES.md` §11 (OFM-010 as sole confirmed gap) point at this registry as the status owner, and CORE_AUDIT gains a CORE-→MIR- mapping for orientation. The documents are kept intact: their reproduction method, execution-verified evidence, the "one self-reinforcing loop" framing and the honest-negatives section are why several fixes took the shape they did, and exist nowhere else. OFM's sections 3–9 were never claims about this repo and are unaffected
- **Original note:** **Do not delete** the docs (history-of-regressions value); refresh their open/closed ledger in a later stage under operator command.

### MIR-037 — ROADMAP / COMMANDS_MAP omit `core/intent_understanding`
- **Aliases:** Stage-1 finding. **Provenance:** newly_discovered.
- **Sources:** DOCUMENT_INVENTORY.md; `core/intent_understanding.py` (commits `9005f6b`/`564e0d8`, 2026-07-18).
- **Symptom:** the model-based intent translator (a distinct layer from `operator_intent`) is absent from ROADMAP Track G and the COMMANDS_MAP NL-routing table.
- **Root cause:** capability landed after the docs were last updated.
- **Status:** **`fixed` (2026-07-20)** ⬆ from `stale_finding`.
- **Resolution:** documented in both, and documented for what it actually is — a **veto**, not a router. `core/intent_understanding` is consulted only after the deterministic matcher already produced a candidate (`main.py:1069`) and can only *cancel* it; it may not choose an action, may only see the kernel's real capability list, and any uncertainty (model error, unparseable output, low confidence, no model) leaves deterministic routing untouched. ROADMAP Track G gains that entry; COMMANDS_MAP gains a "Model-assisted veto" subsection placed outside the routing table precisely because it removes routes rather than adding them.
- **Original note:** AGENT_ANATOMY.md was updated to include it; ROADMAP/COMMANDS_MAP were not.

### MIR-038 — missing per-store zero-delta regression matrix under audit/no-learning
- **Aliases:** MGA-09. **Provenance:** previously_documented (needs-e2e).
- **Sources:** MEMORY_SYSTEM_AUDIT.md §B, §E.
- **Files/functions:** `tests/test_audit_read_only.py` (proves the `:audit` path in isolation; no `source_registry Δ=0` test, no per-mode/per-store live matrix).
- **Status:** `planned_gap` (test-coverage gap). Required before MIR-022 (LearningMode) can be claimed complete.

### MIR-133 — a kill mid-append inside a multi-byte character made a state store unreadable forever
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** newly_discovered (external-field sweep 2026-08-22). **Related:** MIR-125 (bounded tail read — the same file class, the other end).
- **External claim.** Field rule for append-only logs: a process killed mid-append leaves a truncated tail line, and read-side recovery must catch damage PER LINE and keep loading the rest (langgraph #8039 on unenforced write ordering and host-dependent post-crash recovery; anthropics/claude-code #28809 — the same class on a config file; several agent runtimes document «torn tail» as a named corruption mechanism).
- **Local applicability.** 24 live state files on one reader (`core/state_integrity.py`), 26 modules using it. Content is Russian by the operator's standing rule, so rows carry multi-byte characters: `data/episodic_memory.jsonl` is 48% non-ASCII, `procedural_memory.jsonl` 24%, `self_improvement_issues.jsonl` 36%.
- **Local reproduction (measured 2026-08-22).** Appending a typical Russian row and cutting it at each byte: 23 of 248 cut points (~9%) land inside a character. 5 of 6 sampled cuts made the store raise `UnicodeDecodeError` on EVERY subsequent read — not the torn row, the WHOLE FILE, permanently. A single 0xFF from a disk error did the same.
- **Causal mechanism.** `read_state_jsonl_unlocked` decoded the whole file with `p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")` before iterating. The per-row checksum, the quarantine directory and the atomic self-heal — machinery built precisely for damaged rows — sat below that line and never ran. A NUL-filled tail (the classic NTFS unclean-shutdown shape) decoded fine and was handled correctly, which is why this never surfaced: the tested corruption shape was the survivable one.
- **Status:** `fixed` (2026-08-22). Whole-file decoding stays the fast path and is byte-for-byte the old behaviour; on `UnicodeDecodeError` the reader falls back to per-line decoding. A line that does not decode STRICTLY is quarantined whole rather than repaired with replacement characters — rows predating the checksum envelope carry no hash, so a substituted character would pass as a value.
- **Regression proof:** `tests/test_a_torn_multibyte_write_does_not_kill_the_store.py` — four tests: the other rows survive a Cyrillic-split kill; the torn line is quarantined with «utf-8» named as the reason; a disk-level bad byte costs one row of six; and the boundary this repair may not cross — a healthy file is read and left byte-identical, with no quarantine directory created. Both halves break-tested (remove the fallback → 3 red; accept the line lossily instead of quarantining → 1 red).

---

### MIR-134 — huggingface's own words for «out of credits» and «bad token» read as provider health
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** newly_discovered (external-field sweep 2026-08-22). **Related:** MIR-132 (same class, first two providers).
- **External claim.** The «429 classification gap»: retry logic lies when durable and transient failures are separated by error TEXT and the text was written by one vendor. Field guidance for agent rate limiting is per-provider circuit state, not a shared string list.
- **Local applicability.** `_PROVIDER_FALLBACK_ORDER` is `(openai, anthropic, huggingface)` and all three have credentials set. `_KEY_CLASS_TEXT_MARKERS` — the only class that demotes provider health — was written from OpenAI's and Anthropic's phrasings during the MIR-132 repair.
- **Local reproduction (measured 2026-08-22).** Four consecutive ledger failures per message: «You have exceeded your monthly included credits for Inference Providers» → **healthy**; «Authorization header is invalid, use 'Bearer API_TOKEN'» → **healthy**. Both are durable (until the month rolls over / until the operator fixes the token), so the provider would be greeted on every call — MIR-132's 391-retry failure, one provider along. Seven other provider messages classified correctly, including three transient ones that must NOT demote.
- **Causal mechanism.** Coverage was inherited: the vocabulary is provider-SHAPED but stored provider-NEUTRALLY, and nothing required evidence per provider. Adding a provider to the fallback order silently added a hole.
- **Status:** `fixed` (2026-08-22). Two markers added (`included credits`, `authorization header is invalid`), and the structural half: a coverage guard that fails when any provider in `_PROVIDER_FALLBACK_ORDER` has no measured phrase at all.
- **Regression proof:** `tests/test_every_configured_provider_can_be_demoted.py` — ten measured messages across three providers, durable and transient, plus the coverage guard. Break-tested: removing both markers reddens exactly the two huggingface durable cases and nothing else.
- **Not fixed, recorded:** the durable list matches on the bare substring `quota`, so a Gemini-style per-minute limit («Quota exceeded for quota metric …») would park a HEALTHY provider for two hours. Unreachable today — google/gemini is in neither `_PROVIDER_FALLBACK_ORDER` nor `_DEFAULT_PROVIDER_ENV` — and recorded rather than pre-emptively patched, because the fix depends on which vendor is actually added.

### MIR-135 — a daemon that crashes on every tick reports `alive`, and nothing counts the crashes
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** newly_discovered (external-field sweep 2026-08-22). **Related:** MIR-131 (write on the autonomous path, read behind a keyboard), MIR-132.
- **External claim.** Class E of the longitudinal silent-failure taxonomy of a production always-on agent runtime: «declared state ≠ runtime state; diagnosis instruments lie» — 8 incidents, human discovery after 13 hours to 60 days of silence, and ~70% of all silent failures caught by a HUMAN reading output rather than by any automated check (arXiv 2606.14589).
- **Local applicability.** The unattended week has no human reader: Telegram is not built, and the health command is behind a keyboard. Whatever the automatic gates read is the whole of the agent's self-knowledge.
- **Local reproduction (measured 2026-08-22).** A five-minute-old heartbeat whose terminal event is `tick_start` (a tick that began and never returned) or `tick_error` yields `status='alive'`, `interpretation='scheduled_daemon_recent_tick'`, and `_daemon_blocks_next_action() is False` — identical to a heartbeat from a tick that completed. Staleness itself IS reported honestly (1 h alive, 24 h+ stale), so the instrument does not lie about liveness; it has no vocabulary for «alive and accomplishing nothing».
- **Causal mechanism.** Two independent halves. (1) `_daemon_payload` derives its verdict from AGE alone; the `event` field is carried into the payload and dropped before the verdict. (2) `logs/daemon_tick.jsonl` — the only place a per-tick outcome is durably recorded — is written by `_log_tick` and **read by nothing**: an AST/grep sweep finds one writer and zero readers, so no consecutive-failure streak exists anywhere to be counted.
- **Why a single non-terminal heartbeat is NOT the signal.** A fresh `tick_start` is the expected state while a tick is running. Only a STREAK distinguishes «in progress» from «crash loop», and the heartbeat is overwritten each tick, so the streak must be carried forward — the file already carries the dry-run streak this way, so the idiom exists.
- **Status:** `open` — reproduced, not repaired. Deliberately not repaired in the same pass as the two defects above: the fix chooses a threshold (how many incomplete ticks in a row constitute a crash loop) and a consequence (whether it blocks the next action), and both belong in one considered change rather than a reflex.
- **Live history does not show this yet:** 724 tick-log records from 2026-07-28 to 2026-08-22 hold 99 `scheduler_tick` and 99 `tick_complete` with no orphaned starts. That is ~4 ticks/day against a 30-minute schedule, consistent with manual runs before the daemon is installed — the shape is a hypothesis about the coming week, not an observed incident.

### MIR-136 — the resume ladder had no upper age bound, so an unattended week would start by re-answering July's chat
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** newly_discovered (architectural-ballast audit 2026-08-22). **Related:** MIR-039/040 (the orphan half), MIR-020.
- **Symptom (measured live).** `data/runtime_tasks.jsonl` held 14 `resume_checkpoint` rows, every one stopped by `budget_exhausted`, from 2026-07-30 to 2026-08-15. All fourteen were CHAT TURNS, not work: «Answer the question: привет», «что ты чувствуешь когда ты неправ», the deliberate missing-file probe `missing_7f91c.txt`, two duplicate hypothesis questions, and one pasted log line.
- **Root cause.** `reactivate_paused_checkpoints` had only a LOWER bound — the 60-minute cooldown — plus oldest-first ordering and a batch of 3. The ladder is wired live at tick startup (`agent_tick.py:833` `_free_stranded_rows`, under the single-instance lock), so the first ~5 ticks of the unattended week (≈2.5 h) would have spent the refilled budget re-answering July, recorded it as work done, and done so AHEAD of the agent's own choice — the queue step of the tick runs before the self-directed producer.
- **Why this is the old architecture, not a bug in isolation.** These rows are the «human assigns concrete work» model fossilised: chat turns from a conversation that ended weeks ago, holding priority over anything the agent would select for itself.
- **Status:** `fixed` (2026-08-22). `_MAX_RESUMABLE_AGE_HOURS = 72` bounds the ladder from above. The bound is AGE, not content: judging a goal's worth by its text is guessing, while age is a fact — an older checkpoint describes a world that no longer exists. A row past the ceiling becomes terminal `failed` with a named reason rather than staying `paused`, for the same reason the exhausted-attempts branch beside it does: a row that will never run again must stop advertising itself as resumable in `summary()`.
- **State migration (operator's decision, 2026-08-22: retire all fourteen).** `scripts/retire_stale_checkpoints.py` — dry-run by default, backs the store up, re-reads inside the store's own lock. Applied: 14 → `failed`, `pending()` = 0, backup at `data/runtime_tasks.jsonl.20260822T203243Z.bak`. Done before the run rather than left to the ceiling's first pass so the retirement is a reviewable act with the goals written down.
- **Regression proof:** `tests/test_a_stale_checkpoint_is_not_resumed.py` — four tests pinning the boundary from BOTH sides: a month-old checkpoint is not resumed and becomes terminal with a named reason; a 2-hour-old one still is; the 60-minute cooldown still holds. Break-tested: removing the ceiling reddens 2, leaving the stale row `paused` reddens 1.

---

### MIR-137 — the executing half of self-build is reachable only from the CLI
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** newly_discovered (architectural-ballast audit 2026-08-22). **Related:** MIR-131 (write on the autonomous path, read behind a keyboard).
- **Symptom.** The tick produces one self-build proposal per pass into the approval inbox and stops. Nothing on the autonomous path builds anything.
- **Evidence, two independent methods.** (1) `core/self_task_builder.build_coding_task` has exactly one caller in the whole tree: `cli/commands_self_task.py`. (2) The queue dispatches only `_RUNNABLE_TASK_KINDS = frozenset({"auto_run", "resume_checkpoint"})` (`core/autonomous_runtime.py:1059`), so no task kind exists that could reach Stage B through the queue either. `agent_tick.py:1182` states the design in its own comment: «only proposes into the human-gated approval inbox».
- **Consequence for an unattended week.** Proposals accumulate with frozen acceptance tests; the inbox's own TTL (`expire_stale`, 3 days) aborts them; nothing is ever built. This is the largest surviving piece of the «human assigns concrete work» model.
- **Status:** `open` — **operator's decision, 2026-08-22: do NOT wire Stage B to the automaton for this week.** Grounds recorded: the first unattended week must not also be the first week in which the agent applies its own code without a human present; that is a §9 right and a separate risk. Revisit after the week on live data.
- **Not a defect of the repair ladder.** Stage A's contract — a human blesses the yardstick before any implementation exists — is deliberate and documented. What is recorded here is that the second half has no autonomous door, not that the first half is wrong.

---

### MIR-138 — накопленную улику нечем спросить: у архива памяти читателя нет вовсе, у квитанций он есть, но только внутри одного прогона
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** newly_discovered (architectural-ballast audit 2026-08-22). **Related:** MIR-131, MIR-044 (the sink that was legitimately retired), MIR-135.
- **Symptom (ИСПРАВЛЕН 2026-08-25).** `data/tool_receipts.jsonl` — 1946 строк, 1,2 МБ. Утверждение исходной записи «читающей функции нет ни в коде, ни в CLI, ни в тестах» **опровергнуто**: `core/receipt_consumer.matched_evidence_lacks_receipt` читает леджер и ПОНИЖАЕТ вердикт `verified`, когда у улики нет квитанции по этой трассе (`core/verifier_core.py:400`). Леджер доходит туда живым — `core/loop_verification._verification_receipt_kwargs` строит `ToolReceiptLedger(default_receipts_path(root))`, а формы `receipt_ledger=` в коде нет, потому что он передаётся распаковкой словаря; именно на этой форме исходный аудит и промахнулся. Путь автономный: безнадзорный прогон зовёт `self.agent.run(...)` того же `AgentLoop`, в который входит верификация. Право квитанций внесено в `docs/audit/MEMORY_AUTHORITY_MAP.md`.
- **Что от записи осталось верным.** Читатель отвечает на ОДИН вопрос внутри ОДНОГО прогона — «есть ли квитанция по этой трассе». Накопленную за неделю улику по-прежнему нечем спросить: ни команды, ни отчёта. Это и есть незакрытая половина.
- **Урок метода.** «Я не нашёл читателя» ≠ «читателя нет». Греп по имени параметра не видит передачу распаковкой; вывод «нет потребителя» требует двух независимых подтверждений и положительного контроля.
- **Why this is NOT the MIR-044 shape.** Consolidation reports were a recomputable tally of statuses the procedures already held — retiring them lost nothing. Receipts are PRIMARY EVIDENCE of what the agent did, and they cannot be reconstructed. Class E of the silent-failure taxonomy (arXiv 2606.14589) names forensic log inspection as what ended investigations that automated checks never opened.
- **Status:** `open`, classified REFACTOR — not REMOVE. The defect is not that receipts are written; it is that after an unattended week the evidence will exist and there will be no way to ask it anything.
- **Anti-requirement.** Do not delete the store and do not make the writer conditional. «No reader» here means «no question has been posed», not «no value».

- **Вторая ось, замерена 2026-08-24 (F-2).** Тот же класс у ПАМЯТИ, и там он крупнее. `load_archive()` не имеет ни одного вызывающего вне своего модуля; `list_persistent` читает только активную часть. Живой счёт: 125 записей активны, **778 в архиве** — 86 % памяти агента недостижимо ни одной командой. Правило MIR-074 называло архивацию «отодвинем подальше», dormant not destroyed, и это верно ровно до тех пор, пока из сна можно разбудить. Читатель не построен (машинерия, и решение по этому классу за оператором); команда архивации теперь называет цену вслух, а растяжка покраснеет, когда читатель появится.

### MIR-139 — the self-apply lane may rewrite the tests that judge it, and its acceptance signal is those same tests
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** external field digest supplied by the operator 2026-08-23; probed against the code the same day. **Related:** MIR-137 (the lane's other half is human-gated), MIR-100 (the human is the missing gate), MIR-053 (whose closed half lives in a file this lane may edit).
- **External claim, and its strength.** «Self-Authored Verification Is Unreliable in Heuristic Self-Improving Agents» (27 July 2026): when an agent changes its policy AND its own tests/verifier in one act, the self-score can stay near-perfect while real deployment performance degrades. Their SEAL keeps the self-tests but adds a hidden external harness audit the agent cannot read or rewrite, returning only accept/reject; it beat the unprotected variant across six models and three seeds. Strength as supplied: **medium–high for the named mechanism**, preprint, limited environments. Recorded at that strength, not higher.
- **Local applicability — measured, not argued.** `core/self_apply_lane.classify_patch_risk` admits a patch when every file is allowlisted and not denylisted. Probed file by file:

  | file | verdict |
  |---|---|
  | `tests/test_ruff_config.py` (the lint-debt ratchet) | **allowed** |
  | `tests/conftest.py` (the outbound-network deny, MIR-053's closed half) | **allowed** |
  | `core/loop.py` | allowed |
  | `scripts/check_ceo_file_baseline.py`, `scripts/check_function_length_baseline.py` | outside the allowlist |
  | `ruff.toml`, `pytest.ini`, `pyproject.toml` | outside the allowlist |

- **And the acceptance signal is the same suite.** The lane's own contract, from its module docstring: «full pytest -> rollback (red) OR local commit (green)» (`core/self_apply_lane.py:6`, gate 8 at `:493`). So policy, yardstick and acceptance are all inside one authority — the exact configuration the July result names.
- **What is NOT true here, and it bounds the severity.** The ratchet SCRIPTS and every config file are out of reach, so the baselines themselves cannot be edited; and the lane's output still passes through the approval inbox (`self_apply_lane.run`, 27 requests, 15 denied). The failure this entry names is that a test-weakening patch is CLASSIFIED low-risk and admitted, and that the green suite it then produces is not independent evidence.
- **Частичная починка 2026-08-24 — закрыто МОЛЧАНИЕ, не полномочие.** Сделано ровно то, что не требует ни второго судьи, ни ужесточения собственного набора, то есть не нарушает анти-требование ниже.

  `judge_change_risks` (в `core/self_apply_lane.py`) снимает мерку судьи ДО применения патча — после него «до» уже негде взять — и называет риск, когда патч меняет ПОЛИТИКУ (`core/`, `cli/`, `tools/`, `app/`, `api/`) и одновременно ОСЛАБЛЯЕТ существующий файл тестов. Ослабление меряется грубо нарочно: меньше функций `test_*` или меньше `assert`, чем было. Добавленный тест не помечается никогда — им самооценку не поднять, а шумящее предупреждение перестают читать.

  Момент выбран по необратимости, а не по удобству. Человек одобряет ЗАПУСК ленты, а лента коммитит только во временную ветку и никогда не сливает; необратим **слив**, и решает его человек по отчёту. Поэтому раскрытие стоит в отчёте, прямо перед этим решением.

  **И оно доходит до глаз.** Замер того же дня: команда печатала proposal, status, reason, branch, files_changed, rollback_status, commit, rejected_files и next — и НЕ печатала `risks` вовсе. Раскрытие, до которого не доходит взгляд, равно молчанию. Печать добавлена отдельным тестом с ломкой на обе половины: снятие мерки и снятие печати краснят каждое своё.

  Формулировка риска называет и числа, и следствие: «judge tests/X was WEAKENED in the same patch as policy (core/Y): tests 2 -> 1, asserts 3 -> 1. The green suite below was produced BY this weakened judge and is not independent evidence».

- **Status:** `open` — и остаётся открытым сознательно. Закрыто молчание; НЕ закрыто то, что запись называет главным: политика, аршин и приёмка по-прежнему внутри одной власти. Ответ поля — приёмочная граница ВНЕ досягаемости агента — не построен, потому что это изменение границы собственных полномочий агента, то есть решение оператора.

  **Решение оператора 2026-08-24, на прямой вопрос с тремя вариантами:** оставить раскрытие, границу полномочий перед безнадзорной неделей не трогать. Отвергнуты запрет ослаблять тесты вместе с кодом (сломал бы честные случаи: слияние шести тестов в один параметризованный тоже убавляет числа) и вынос части тестов в недосягаемый каталог (надёжнее всего, но требует отдельно решить, ЧТО именно выносить). Основание выбора: на эту неделю агент ветки не сливает сам — каждый слив смотрит человек, и раскрытие даёт ему недостающий факт. Живые случаи за неделю станут материалом для решения по существу, вместо препринта.
- **Anti-requirement.** Do not answer this by making the agent's own suite stricter: a stricter self-authored yardstick is still self-authored. The field's answer is an acceptance boundary OUTSIDE the agent's reach, and the cheapest local approximation is that the ratchet scripts and configs already are — the gap is `tests/` itself.

- **Вторая ось, замерена 2026-08-24 (H-39).** `_is_allowed` пропускает не только судей, но и `docs/audit/` — то есть журнал исторических отказов и сам этот реестр. Подсудимый может править дело. Оси разные по последствиям: правка судьи меняет БУДУЩИЕ вердикты, правка реестра меняет ПРОШЛЫЕ улики. Ограничитель в обоих случаях один и он не в пути: одобрение, тесты и история git, причём `.git/` закрыт префиксом. Замер приложен сюда, а не заведён новой записью, потому что дверь одна.

### MIR-140 — the verify-replan loop buys its own acceptance: it fetches the URLs the draft cited, and the verdict follows resolution
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** field digest supplied by the operator 2026-08-23 («Verify, Repair, Repeat, or Stop?», arXiv 2607.17641, 20 July 2026); probed against the code and the live logs the same day. **Related:** MIR-060 (**the same wound, already banked** — the verdict follows citation resolution, not the claim's truth), MIR-016.
- **External claim.** A verify → repair → verify loop does not improve monotonically: the repairer can damage an already-correct plan, and «reported acceptance keeps rising while true validity falls». The remedy offered is VRR-Stop (commit or repair by the sign of the true marginal gain) with VRR-Guard as an estimation-free fallback when verifier discrimination approaches zero — replace the incumbent candidate only under a sufficient verification margin. Reported: +60.6 pp true validity over fixed five-round repair at 0.72 repairs on a GSM8K stress setting. Strength as supplied: medium–high; a model of verify-repair, not a week of a full agent.
- **The paper's CENTRAL failure does not reach us, and the reason is worth keeping.** Our verify-driven replan is **evidence-additive, not answer-repairing**: it re-plans in order to `web_fetch` the sources the draft could not resolve, then calls `_verify(answer=st.draft_answer, chain=st.chain, ...)` — **the ORIGINAL draft**, against an enriched chain (`core/loop_verify_replan.py:~399`). There is no repairer of the answer on this path, so a correct candidate cannot be damaged by one. The incumbent is retained by construction, which is the property VRR-Guard exists to impose elsewhere.
- **The stopping remedy targets a cost we barely pay.** Across 604 live traces: `replan` 7, `replan_attempt` 7, `replan_exhausted` 8, `verify_replan_noop` 1. The global cap is 3 (`DEFAULT_MAX_TOTAL_REPLANS`), not five, and it is reached in roughly 1% of runs. Stopping earlier than a fixed five rounds is not our problem.
- **But the paper's OTHER half lands squarely, by a different mechanism than theirs.** The loop's repair action is to fetch **the very URLs the draft already cited** — the code says so in its own words: «The draft already cites these URLs (that's why they were unresolved); now the chain has the web_page evidence so `match_citation` will resolve them.» And MIR-060, banked as an xfail since before this, states that the verdict follows citation RESOLUTION and never whether the claim follows from the source. Put together: the loop mechanically raises acceptance without touching truth. «Reported acceptance keeps rising while true validity falls» is not a risk we might import — it is the shape of the loop we already run.
- **A sensor reading that must not be misused, recorded because it nearly was.** The `verify` event is the most frequent in the logs (1081 across 604 traces) and is 99.8% `ok=True` (1079 / 2). It is NOT the answer verifier: it is `tool.validate_output` on a step's output (`core/loop_step_execution.py:530`). It says nothing about verifier discrimination, and anyone reaching for it as evidence of a permissive verifier is measuring a different organ.
- **Status:** `open` as a FRAME, not as new work. It adds no repair of its own: the actionable defect is MIR-060, which already exists and is already banked with a test. What this entry contributes is why MIR-060 matters more than its severity suggested — it is not one gate being lenient, it is the thing that makes our only verify-repair loop an acceptance-raising machine.
- **Anti-requirement.** Do not import VRR-Stop's estimator. It needs verifier false-accept / false-reject rates we have never measured, and the paper itself says that when discrimination approaches zero the estimate can flip the stopping sign. Measuring discrimination comes first; a stopping rule built on unmeasured parameters would be a control in appearance only.

- **ТЕНЬ ПРОЧИТАНА 2026-08-25, и она отвечает на вопрос оператора от 27 июля.** Тогда было решено: сигнал застоя (`TerminationGuard`, MAST FM-1.5) записывает, ГДЕ остановка произошла бы, и НЕ останавливает — чтобы сперва узнать, что она стоила бы или сберегла. Месяц собранные улики никто не читал; ответ лежал в журналах.

  Тень сработала **три раза**, и во всех трёх `artifacts_at_detection` пуст:

  | попытка | коды отказа | было в руках |
  |---|---|---|
  | 2 | `plan_parse_failed` ×2 | — |
  | 2 | `plan_parse_failed` ×2 | — |
  | 3 | `verify_failed` ×2 | — |

  **Остановка не стоила бы ничего и не сберегла бы ничего.** Работы в руках не было ни разу, а бюджеты по видам отказов остановили цикл на ТОЙ ЖЕ попытке во всех трёх случаях: у `plan_parse_failed` и `verify_failed` потолок 2, глобальный потолок 3.

- **Поэтому сигнал НЕ повышен до управляющего, и это измерение, а не осторожность.** Новое поле (Bayesian Partner Modelling, 19 августа; ScienceFlow/ESTRA, 14 августа) убедительно показывает, что ценность даёт переход управления, а не знание: у Bayesian триггер по противоречию дал 2 перепланирования против 43/169/119 у прочих при сопоставимой награде. Но у нас такой триггер уже есть — им служат бюджеты по ВИДАМ отказов, то есть «этот вид ремонта не работает — стоп», и повышение тени завело бы вторую власть над решением, которым владеет `ReplanPolicy.decide`. Граница закреплена `tests/test_the_stagnation_signal_is_still_observational.py`, и ломка проверена: `break` по сигналу застоя краснит тест.

- **Свежий замер живого распределения, 2026-08-25.** Цепочек перепланирования 11, из них **10 закончились исчерпанием**; в **7 из 10** один и тот же код отказа встречался дважды. То есть наше правило остановки на деле и есть «тот же отказ повторился — стоп», выраженное счётчиками: `plan_parse_failed: 2`, `verify_failed: 2`, `unresolved_citation: 2` (×4), `file_not_found: 1`, `injection_blocked: 1`. Отдельного правила по ожидаемому приросту строить не из чего — оно совпало бы с уже действующим.

### MIR-141 — verifier discrimination measured: perfect on form, blind on content, and one false-rejection channel found and fixed
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** operator instruction 2026-08-23 («мерь дискриминацию верификатора»), which MIR-140 named as the prerequisite before any stopping rule. **Related:** MIR-060 (this is its number), MIR-140, MIR-016.
- **Method.** Paired probes, the shape the field uses: for each class a VALID answer and its INVALID twin differing in exactly one element, the valid one a verbatim restatement of the source with a correct citation. 40 pairs per class, structural pass only (`llm=None`), so the measurement is deterministic and costs nothing. Discrimination reported as J = accept-rate(valid) − accept-rate(invalid).
- **THE MEASUREMENT (after the repair below):**

  | class | accepts valid | accepts INVALID | J |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | B. the cited source exists | 100% | 0% | **+1.00** |
  | D. a citation is present at all | 100% | 0% | **+1.00** |
  | A. the number matches the source | 100% | 75% | **+0.25** |
  | C. the claim is on the chain's topic | 100% | 100% | **0.00** |
  | E. the polarity matches the source | 100% | 100% | **0.00** |

- **The shape is unambiguous: the verifier is perfect on FORM and blind on CONTENT.** Is there a citation, does the cited source exist — caught every time. Does the claim follow, is it even about the same subject, does it assert what the source asserts or its negation — not caught. A sentence that says «— неверно, это не так» about its own source verifies at 100%. **This is MIR-060's number**, and it is what makes the verify-replan loop an acceptance-raising machine (MIR-140): the loop fetches the cited URL, resolution succeeds, and resolution is essentially all the verdict measures.
- **A FALSE-REJECTION channel found by the same measurement, and fixed.** Before the repair, acceptance of VALID answers sat at exactly 80% in every class — and that flatness is what gave the cause away. Isolated: the same sentence, supported by the same source, flips verdict on a digit in the citation ADDRESS — `[web:…/p/2]` verified, `[web:…/p/10]` demoted; `/abc` verified, `/999` demoted. Root: `extract_statistical_figures` received the chunk text WITH its citation marker, so digits from the URL path entered the claim's figure list (`…20 миллисекунд. [web:…/p/10]` → `['20', '10']`); the extra figure is absent from the source, so a correctly cited claim was demoted to `topic_supported_but_claim_unverified`. Single-character tails passed only by falling under `_STAT_FIGURE_MIN_LEN`, so the damage grew with identifier length — and real URLs are full of digits: dates `/2026/08/23/`, article ids, page numbers. The demotion feeds `unverified_total` in the low-evidence policy and neighbours the verify-replan triggers, so it cost accuracy AND spare repairs.
- **Status of the repair:** `fixed` (2026-08-23). The citation marker is stripped before figures are extracted. Pinned by `tests/test_a_citation_address_is_not_a_claim_figure.py` — five real URL shapes, the root pinned separately from its effect, and two boundaries: figures in the prose itself are still extracted, and a substituted number is still caught. Break-tested (restore the address to the scan → 7 red).
- **And the repair made the verifier look WORSE, which is the honest part.** Class C fell from +0.55 to 0.00 and class A from a measured 0.05 to +0.25 only because valid acceptance rose to 100%. The old apparent discrimination on off-topic claims was an ARTIFACT of the defect: those invalid twins were being rejected by the URL-digit accident, not by any check that understood them. Removing a defect removed a source of accidental correctness, and the verifier's real content-blindness became visible.
- **Status:** `open` for the discrimination itself, `fixed` for the false-rejection channel. What the number licenses: MIR-140's anti-requirement now has evidence — an estimator-based stopping rule (VRR-Stop) must NOT be imported, because on the two axes that matter most, discrimination is exactly zero, and the paper itself says a near-zero-discrimination verifier flips the stopping sign. What it forbids: treating a `verified` verdict as evidence that a claim is true. It is evidence that a citation resolved.

### MIR-142 — content blindness: the topic axis closed, the polarity axis measured and left open
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** operator instruction 2026-08-23 («почини содержательную слепоту»), acting on MIR-141's measurement. **Related:** MIR-141 (the number), MIR-060 (the class), MIR-140.
- **What was closed.** A seventh content gate (`off_topic_reason`, `core/verifier_absence.py`, wired in `verifier_core`'s verdict ladder): a resolved citation must be ABOUT the claim. Topic axis **J 0.00 → +1.00**, valid acceptance unchanged at **100%**. Re-measurable by `scripts/measure_verifier_discrimination.py`.
- **Why the five existing gates were silent, which is the part worth keeping.** The ladder judged literals, and `_SALIENT_LITERAL_RE` recognises only code-shaped ones — snake_case, hashes, versions, filenames. `salient_literals("Курс акций компании вырос на 20 процентов")` is the EMPTY SET. The content gates were built for engineering claims about files and identifiers and are structurally mute on prose, which is most of what an agent answering an operator in Russian produces.
- **Two design decisions, both bought with the corpus rather than with caution.**
  1. **It demotes without accusing.** The first version returned a `ClaimReason`, and in this ladder a reason means REFUTED — proven false. A citation that is not about the claim does not make the claim false. **Seventeen existing verifier fixtures turned `refuted`** before this was corrected; the chunk now goes to topic-only.
  2. **It judges only QUANTITATIVE claims.** The rule «demote on zero shared content words» was right in the artificial case and wrong on the corpus: it demoted (a) a **Russian claim citing an English excerpt**, where the words differ by language and not by subject — and that is this agent's daily shape, it reads English and answers in Russian; and (b) a **meta-claim about the evidence** («Both files agree»), whose words describe a relationship while the excerpt is content. Requiring a statistical figure restricts the gate to claims concrete enough to owe their source something.
- **What is left OPEN, measured and banked rather than pretended away.** A qualitative off-topic claim («Совет директоров одобрил новую дивидендную политику» cited to a latency benchmark) still verifies. Banked as a strict xfail in `tests/test_a_resolved_citation_must_be_about_the_claim.py` carrying the two false-rejection classes above as its grounds. **Deterministic judgement of a qualitative claim is not available here**: the honest instrument is a semantic check, i.e. a model call per verification, i.e. money and an operator's decision.
- **The polarity axis is untouched and its number stands at J = 0.00.** A sentence that repeats its source and then says «— неверно, это не так» verifies at 100%. A deterministic gate was considered and NOT built: distinguishing «the source refutes X» from «I refute the source's X» requires knowing what the source asserts, and a naive negation-marker rule would manufacture exactly the false-rejection class this entry just spent its evidence avoiding.
- **Status:** `partially_fixed`. Topic axis closed and pinned; polarity axis open with its number; qualitative topic judgement open with its grounds. The `verify` length ratchet was raised by the measured 7 lines with those grounds recorded, and the story moved to `docs/CODE_NOTES.md` («A citation that is not about the claim») rather than growing the function further.
- **Anti-requirement, carried forward from MIR-140 and now doubly grounded.** Do not wire the existing optional NLI pass as the answer to this: by its own contract it only UPGRADES `cited_but_unmatched` chunks to verified, so it would lower discrimination, not raise it. It is a recall device, not a gate.

### MIR-143 — the number axis sits at J = +0.25 because the COMPUTING gate is silent, not because a presence rule is missing
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** operator instruction 2026-08-23 («мерь ось числа»). **Related:** MIR-141 (the measurement), MIR-142 (the topic axis, closed), MIR-060.
- **The sensor decomposed first, and the number came out exact.** Of the four templates in the discrimination probe, figures were extracted from **one**: `_STAT_TRIGGER_RE` calls a claim statistical only on a percentage, a currency, a range, a multiple, or a qualifier word («средний», «average», «most»). The other three — «Выручка … 999 миллионов рублей», «На складе B2 … 999 единиц», «В проекте участвуют 999 инженеров» — yield `[]`, so no numeric check runs at all and the substituted number verifies. One caught of four is exactly the measured **J = +0.25**.
- **A presence rule was built, measured, and REVERTED.** An eighth gate («a cited number must appear in the cited evidence») lifted the axis to **J = +1.00** with valid acceptance still 100%, and the full battery held at 8603 passing. It was reverted anyway, for two reasons that only appeared under measurement:
  1. **It cost measured capability.** `tests/test_capability_baseline.py` fell **29 → 27**. The two losses were `arithmetic-07` («117 tests passed» against `tests_total=120, tests_failed=3` — correct and DERIVED, so 117 is nowhere in the excerpt) and `inference-08` («a version below 3.0» against `version=2.11.0` — a comparison BOUND, not a quotation). Both are legitimate claims, and nothing on the bench improved to pay for them.
  2. **It violated a boundary this repository had already reasoned out and written down.** `core/verifier_absence.py` states it in place: bare numbers and decimals are deliberately excluded from literals, because «счёт, сумма и сравнение принадлежат `evaluate_claim_arithmetic`, и второй судья над той же областью спорил бы с первым». The presence gate is precisely that second judge. Yielding to the arithmetic gate on `supports` did not rescue it either — measured, both tasks come back `silent`, so there was nothing to yield to.
- **So the defect is located one layer down, and named.** The number axis is not missing a presence check; the COMPUTING check is silent on ordinary shapes it should own. «120 total minus 3 failed = 117» is arithmetic, and «2.11.0 < 3.0» is a comparison — both are `evaluate_claim_arithmetic`'s declared business, and on both it returns `silent`. A checker that refuses to judge costs a missed defect; that trade is stated in the module's own docstring and is the right one, but the set of shapes it recognises is narrower than the set it claims.
- **Status:** `open`, with its red witnesses already written by someone else: `arithmetic-07` and `inference-08` on the capability bench are exactly the shapes to teach the computing gate, and both are green today only because nothing checks them. Teaching the gate raises the axis without adding a second judge.
- **Anti-requirement.** Do not close this axis with a presence rule, however well it measures on paired probes. It scored +1.00 and still lost two real tasks, and the repository had already ruled on why.

### MIR-144 — the polarity axis closed, and a false-rejection channel I had introduced the same hour, found and closed with it
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** operator instruction 2026-08-23 («закрой ось полярности»). **Related:** MIR-141 (the measurement), MIR-142 (the topic gate — whose regression this entry repairs), MIR-060.
- **Polarity: J 0.00 → +1.00**, valid acceptance unchanged at 100%. A ninth gate (`denies_own_evidence_reason`): a claim cannot lean on evidence it declares false.
- **What made it possible after it had been refused.** The refusal on 2026-08-23 was correct for the rule considered then — «claim contains a negation, demote» — which Russian defeats immediately («не превысила», «не менее», «а не пиковая»). Two changes make the rule computable: polarity is compared **between both texts** rather than searched in one, and only **META-denial** counts — «неверно», «это не так», «is not true», «is false» — a denial of the whole proposition, not of a sentence member. Both are lexicon, the same device `claim_arithmetic` uses, and for the same stated reason: judging by resemblance is what must not happen.
- **The boundary that makes it honest:** if the excerpt itself carries a denial, the claim is REPORTING it and there is nothing to demote. Break-tested — removing that symmetry reddens the reporting case immediately.
- **A guard that was NOT proven, and was then proven.** The «shared subject» condition survived its break-test untouched, meaning nothing pinned it — an unpinned guard is indistinguishable from dead code. It now has a direct unit test: the gate must stay silent when a denial is about something the evidence does not discuss (that is the seventh gate's business, not this one).
- **AND THE REAL FIND OF THIS PASS, which was not what was asked for.** Building the polarity probe surfaced that **MIR-142's own topic gate, shipped an hour earlier, was demoting two of three CORRECT Russian claims cited to an English source** — the agent's daily shape, since it reads English and answers in Russian. The figure requirement I had relied on to prevent exactly this does not prevent it: the figure is what ENABLES the gate. Two causes, both fixed:
  1. **Different alphabets are not different subjects.** The gate now stays silent when claim and excerpt are in different dominant scripts. Cross-language word overlap does not exist, so there is nothing to judge.
  2. **The gate used the tokeniser that does NOT apply the stoplist.** `topic_tokens` keeps «the» and «was»; `discriminating_tokens` removes them. With the wrong one, an English claim shared «the» with any English excerpt and the gate was mute on every English mismatch.
- **Status:** `fixed` for polarity, and for the MIR-142 regression. Pinned by `tests/test_a_claim_may_not_deny_its_own_source.py` (10 cases: three denial shapes, the reporting boundary, three ordinary negations, English within English, and the subject guard) and by the cross-language cases added to `tests/test_a_resolved_citation_must_be_about_the_claim.py`.
- **A miss recorded, not chased:** an English quantitative off-topic claim still verifies, because `_STAT_TRIGGER_RE` knows `%` and «процент» but not the English word «percent», so the seventh gate never arms. That is a MISSED detection rather than a false rejection, and it belongs to MIR-143's finding that the trigger is narrow and language-asymmetric.
- **The lesson this entry exists to carry.** The regression was found only because a probe for a DIFFERENT axis happened to include a cross-language case, and its verdict looked right for the wrong reason. A gate shipped with a green battery an hour earlier was already harming the most common shape in production, and no test covered it because no fixture combined a figure with two languages. Measuring one thing is how the other was caught.

### MIR-145 — the citation-number axis closed by a NAMED shape, honouring the anti-requirement that forbade a presence rule
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** operator instruction 2026-08-23 («закрой ось цитаты-числа»). **Related:** MIR-143 (whose anti-requirement constrained this), MIR-141, MIR-144.
- **The axis: J +0.25 → +1.00**, valid acceptance unchanged at 100%, capability bench unchanged at **29 / 17**. All seven measured axes now read +1.00.
- **The constraint this had to satisfy.** MIR-143 forbids closing this axis with a presence rule («every number in a claim must appear in the evidence»): that rule scored +1.00 and still cost two bench tasks, because `117` in «117 tests passed» is DERIVED from 120−3 and `3.0` in «version below 3.0» is a comparison BOUND — both legitimately absent. The gate built here is a named shape instead: **a claim that restates a sentence of its evidence almost verbatim may not change the number.** When the only difference is the number, the number is the only thing the claim adds of its own.
- **The distinction is not asserted, it is pinned.** The boundary tests are exactly the two shapes the presence rule broke — a derived number and a comparison bound both stay verified — plus a paraphrase in different words, which the form deliberately does not judge.
- **A pre-existing false rejection fixed on the way.** «Средняя задержка составила около 20 миллисекунд» against an excerpt reading 19.8 was already being demoted by the older statistical gate. Rather than exempt approximations — which would have been a miss, since «около 20» against 500 must still be caught — the gate gained a computed **10% tolerance**, and both sides are pinned.
- **Two guards that survived their break-tests, and what that revealed.** Both were unpinned, i.e. indistinguishable from dead code, and pinning them found a real flaw:
  1. The approximation skip did nothing — because the **sentence splitter cut on the decimal point**, so «19.8» became «19» and «8», the evidence sentence was never matched, and the gate stayed silent on any excerpt containing a decimal. Fixed with a splitter that does not break between digits.
  2. The splitter fix itself then still did not redden end-to-end, because the statistical gate intercepts that particular case first. It is pinned directly on the function instead, with a non-statistical sentence carrying a decimal — a break there now reddens.
- **Status:** `fixed`. Pinned by `tests/test_a_restatement_may_not_change_the_number.py` (14 cases). `verify`'s length ratchet raised by the measured 9 lines with its grounds recorded.
- **What the seven +1.00 readings do NOT mean.** They are the discrimination of the STRUCTURAL pass over the shapes the probe names, not a claim that the verifier understands claims. The banked xfail from MIR-142 still stands: a QUALITATIVE off-topic claim verifies, and no deterministic gate here can judge it. The number to distrust is any future one that reads +1.00 on an axis nobody constructed a probe for.

### MIR-146 — the banked gap closed: a qualitative off-topic claim is no longer verified
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** operator instruction 2026-08-23 («закрой качественное утверждение не по теме»). **Related:** MIR-142 (which banked this gap), MIR-144 (whose repair removed the first blocker), MIR-143.
- **What was banked, and why it could be closed now.** MIR-142 restricted the topic gate to QUANTITATIVE claims and banked the qualitative case as a strict xfail, with two measured false-rejection classes as its grounds. The figure requirement was a blunt stand-in for distinctions nobody had yet made. It is now removed, and four distinctions stand in its place — each one named by the corpus rather than by argument:
  1. **Cross-language** (a Russian claim citing an English excerpt). Closed already by MIR-144's script guard; that repair is what made this attempt possible.
  2. **Meta-claims about the evidence** — «both files agree», «the three values sum to 6». Their vocabulary describes an OPERATION over the excerpt, not its subject, so word divergence proves nothing. A meta-vocabulary set makes the gate silent on them, and the gate additionally **yields to the computing gate** whenever arithmetic already SUPPORTS the claim.
  3. **Evidence that is not prose.** For `file`, `test`, `shell`, `log` and `diff` the excerpt is code and structured lines; comparing a sentence's words against it is meaningless. Worse, the gate was **stealing the verdict from a more specific one** — a chunk with no receipt came back `topic_only` instead of `receipt_missing`. The gate now judges prose sources only (`web`, `search`).
  4. **Derived claims**, which have their own judge (see 2).
- **Measured at every step, and the corpus was the arbiter.** Lifting the figure requirement broke **8** tests; the meta-vocabulary guard and the arithmetic yield took it to **3**; restricting to prose sources took it to **0**. At each step the failures were read for their CLASS rather than patched away — the receipt-missing case in particular was not a false rejection but a stolen verdict, which is a different defect and would have been invisible if the tests had simply been adjusted.
- **Status:** `fixed`. The strict xfail is converted into an enforced test, per the repository's own rule that a banked gap is replaced when it XPASSes. Capability bench unchanged at **29 / 17**; battery 8641; all seven measured axes at **+1.00**.
- **The honest boundary that remains, stated so nobody reads +1.00 as understanding.** The gate fires only when a prose claim shares **zero** content words with a prose excerpt. A claim that is off-topic while sharing one incidental word still verifies, and no probe in the suite measures that. What was closed is the extreme case; what remains open has no number, which is the more dangerous condition — an axis nobody constructed a probe for reads as +1.00 by default.

### MIR-147 — the unmeasured axis measured: one incidental shared word reads J = 0.00, and no lexical threshold can close it
- **Aliases:** none. **Provenance:** operator instruction 2026-08-23 («построй пробу на одно случайное общее слово»), acting on the boundary MIR-146 declared but did not measure. **Related:** MIR-146, MIR-142, MIR-141.
- **Why this probe existed to be built.** MIR-146 closed the extreme case — zero shared content words — and said plainly that a claim off-topic while sharing one incidental word still verifies, with no probe measuring it. An axis nobody probes reads +1.00 by default, and that is the more dangerous state.
- **The probe (class H in `scripts/measure_verifier_discrimination.py`), proved before it was trusted.** Four excerpt/off-topic pairs where the shared word is the kind that drifts across topics in real text — «обработки», «квартал», «проекте», «запись». Each pair was checked to share EXACTLY one non-numeric content token before being used: the first draft had one pair sharing zero, which would have measured class C under class H's name.
- **THE FIRST READING WAS +0.25, AND IT WAS MY OWN DEFECT.** Decomposing which quarter was caught showed the gate firing on «Запись концерта выложили» for the wrong reason: `запись` sits in the meta-vocabulary list, and the gate SUBTRACTED meta words from the claim, artificially emptying the overlap. «Запись», «источник», «результат», «строка», «файл» are ordinary Russian nouns; subtracting them from a claim is a false-rejection channel. The subtraction is removed — the meta list now answers only «is the whole chunk about the evidence» — and the battery stayed green, so it had never helped anywhere. **The honest reading is J = 0.00.**
- **Then the axis was attacked properly, with a threshold sweep, and the result is an impossibility rather than a tuning:**

  | overlap threshold | class H | cost |
  |---|---|---|
  | zero (shipped) | **0.00** | none |
  | 0.20 | +0.25 | demotes two longer TRUE paraphrases (overlap 0.12 and 0.14) |
  | 0.34 | +1.00 | demotes «Квартальный доход достиг 20 миллионов» — a correct restatement |
  | 0.50 | +1.00 | two corpus fixtures |

- **The reason no threshold works, stated as the measurement shows it.** The longer and more natural a TRUE paraphrase, the lower its word-overlap ratio — the same profile an off-topic claim has. «За отчётный период организация получила доход в размере 20 миллионов» shares one token of seven with «Выручка за квартал составила 20 миллионов рублей», and it is correct. Lexical overlap cannot separate «different words, same subject» from «one word in common, different subject»; that distinction is semantic.
- **Status:** `open`, and open with a number instead of a silence. Zero-overlap stays. Three long paraphrases are pinned as regression tests so a future threshold cannot creep back in unmeasured, and the sweep is recorded in the code beside the rule.
- **What would actually close it**, stated without pretending it is free: a semantic check — a model call per verification. That is money and an operator's decision, and MIR-140's anti-requirement applies to any estimator built on top of it: the discrimination of the semantic pass would itself have to be measured before anything is allowed to depend on it.
